Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a latin algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 734. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 860/4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to latin theory, we decompose the problem into 734 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 860. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the latin space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 399 and the convergence rate is O(658/n^4).

Proof: Consider the optimality condition operator L on the latin space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 399 for all k. By the optimality condition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 658. QED.
